functions: fst/1, pair/2, pk/1, sign/2, snd/1, true/0, verify/3

rule (modulo E) Register:
   [ Fr( ~sk ) ]
  --[ Register( $s ), OnlyOnce( <'reg', $s> ) ]->
   [ !Seat( $s, ~sk ), !Pk( $s, pk(~sk) ), Out( pk(~sk) ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

restriction SeatUniverse:
  "∀ s #i.
    (Register( s ) @ #i) ⇒
    ((((s = 's0') ∨ (s = 's1')) ∨ (s = 's2')) ∨ (s = 's3'))"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ s #i.
    (Register( s ) @ #i) ⇒
    ((((s = 's0') ∨ (s = 's1')) ∨ (s = 's2')) ∨ (s = 's3'))"
  */

restriction OnlyOnce:
  "∀ x #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( x ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( x ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ x #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( x ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( x ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  */

rule (modulo E) Corrupt:
   [ !Seat( $s, ~sk ) ] --[ Corrupt( $s ) ]-> [ Out( ~sk ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Sign:
   [ !Seat( $s, ~sk ), In( <K, v> ) ]
  --[ Signed( $s, K, v ) ]->
   [ Out( sign(<K, v, $s>, ~sk) ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

restriction HonestSignsOneCandidatePerCoordinate:
  "∀ s K v1 v2 #i #j.
    ((Signed( s, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Signed( s, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ⇒ (v1 = v2)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ s K v1 v2 #i #j.
    ((Signed( s, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Signed( s, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ⇒ (v1 = v2)"
  */

rule (modulo E) Accept_012[no_derivcheck]:
   [
   !Pk( 's0', pk(skA) ), !Pk( 's1', pk(skB) ), !Pk( 's2', pk(skC) ),

rule (modulo E) Extract:

lemma sanity_honest_certificate:
  exists-trace
  "∃ c K v #i. (Accept( c, K, v ) @ #i) ∧ (¬(∃ s #k. Corrupt( s ) @ #k))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ c K v #i. (Accept( c, K, v ) @ #i) ∧ ∀ s #k. (Corrupt( s ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥"
*/

solve( Accept( c, K, v ) @ #i )
  case Accept_012
  solve( !Pk( 's0', pk(skA) ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Pk( 's1', pk(skB) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Pk( 's2', pk(skC) ) ▶₂ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !KU( sign(<K, v, 's0'>, ~sk) ) @ #vk.5 )
          case Sign
          solve( !KU( sign(<K, v, 's1'>, ~sk.1) ) @ #vk.8 )
            case Sign
            solve( !KU( sign(<K, v, 's2'>, ~sk.2) ) @ #vk.9 )
              case Sign
              SOLVED // trace found
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma A1_three_distinct_signers:
  all-traces
  "∀ c K v #i.
    (Accept( c, K, v ) @ #i) ⇒
    (∃ a1 a2 a3.
      ((((((Signers( c, a1, a2, a3 ) @ #i) ∧ (Verified( a1, K, v ) @ #i)) ∧
          (Verified( a2, K, v ) @ #i)) ∧
         (Verified( a3, K, v ) @ #i)) ∧
        (¬(a1 = a2))) ∧
       (¬(a1 = a3))) ∧
      (¬(a2 = a3)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ c K v #i.
  (Accept( c, K, v ) @ #i)
 ∧
  ∀ a1 a2 a3.
   (Signers( c, a1, a2, a3 ) @ #i) ∧
   (Verified( a1, K, v ) @ #i) ∧
   (Verified( a2, K, v ) @ #i) ∧
   (Verified( a3, K, v ) @ #i)
  ⇒
   ((a1 = a2) ∨ (a1 = a3) ∨ (a2 = a3))"
*/
simplify
solve( Accept( c, K, v ) @ #i )
  case Accept_012
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
next
  case Accept_013
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
next
  case Accept_023
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
next
  case Accept_123
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
qed

                case Corrupt
                solve( !KU( sign(<K, v, 's3'>, ~sk.2) ) @ #vk.12 )
                  case Sign
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                next
                  case c_sign
                  solve( !KU( ~sk.2 ) @ #vk.16 )
                    case Corrupt
                    by contradiction /* from formulas */
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          next
            case c_sign
            solve( !KU( ~sk ) @ #vk.11 )
              case Corrupt
              solve( !KU( sign(<K, v, 's2'>, ~sk.1) ) @ #vk.10 )
                case Sign
                solve( !KU( sign(<K, v, 's3'>, ~sk.2) ) @ #vk.12 )

                  by contradiction /* from formulas */

lemma A_acceptance_authorized:
  all-traces
  "∀ c K v #i.
    (Accept( c, K, v ) @ #i) ⇒
    (∃ a1 a2 a3.
      (((((((((Signers( c, a1, a2, a3 ) @ #i) ∧ (Verified( a1, K, v ) @ #i)) ∧
             (Verified( a2, K, v ) @ #i)) ∧
            (Verified( a3, K, v ) @ #i)) ∧
           (¬(a1 = a2))) ∧
          (¬(a1 = a3))) ∧
         (¬(a2 = a3))) ∧
        ((∃ #k. (Signed( a1, K, v ) @ #k) ∧ (#k < #i)) ∨
         (∃ #k. (Corrupt( a1 ) @ #k) ∧ (#k < #i)))) ∧
       ((∃ #k. (Signed( a2, K, v ) @ #k) ∧ (#k < #i)) ∨
        (∃ #k. (Corrupt( a2 ) @ #k) ∧ (#k < #i)))) ∧
      ((∃ #k. (Signed( a3, K, v ) @ #k) ∧ (#k < #i)) ∨
       (∃ #k. (Corrupt( a3 ) @ #k) ∧ (#k < #i))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ c K v #i.
  (Accept( c, K, v ) @ #i)
 ∧
  ∀ a1 a2 a3.
   (Signers( c, a1, a2, a3 ) @ #i) ∧
   (Verified( a1, K, v ) @ #i) ∧
   (Verified( a2, K, v ) @ #i) ∧
   (Verified( a3, K, v ) @ #i)
  ⇒
   ((a1 = a2) ∨
    (a1 = a3) ∨
    (a2 = a3) ∨
    ((∀ #k. (Signed( a1, K, v ) @ #k) ⇒ ¬(#k < #i)) ∧
     (∀ #k. (Corrupt( a1 ) @ #k) ⇒ ¬(#k < #i))) ∨
    ((∀ #k. (Signed( a2, K, v ) @ #k) ⇒ ¬(#k < #i)) ∧
     (∀ #k. (Corrupt( a2 ) @ #k) ⇒ ¬(#k < #i))) ∨
    ((∀ #k. (Signed( a3, K, v ) @ #k) ⇒ ¬(#k < #i)) ∧
     (∀ #k. (Corrupt( a3 ) @ #k) ⇒ ¬(#k < #i))))"
*/
simplify
solve( Accept( c, K, v ) @ #i )

lemma D2_intersection_seats_corrupt [reuse]:
  all-traces
  "∀ s c1 c2 K v1 v2 #i #j.
    (((((Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ∧
       (¬(v1 = v2))) ∧
      (Verified( s, K, v1 ) @ #i)) ∧
     (Verified( s, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ⇒
    (∃ #k. (Corrupt( s ) @ #k) ∧ ((#k < #i) ∨ (#k < #j)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ s c1 c2 K v1 v2 #i #j.
  (Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧
  (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j) ∧
  (Verified( s, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧
  (Verified( s, K, v2 ) @ #j)
 ∧
  (¬(v1 = v2)) ∧ (∀ #k. (Corrupt( s ) @ #k) ⇒ (¬(#k < #i)) ∧ (¬(#k < #j)))"
*/
simplify
solve( (∃ #k. (Signed( s, K, v1 ) @ #k) ∧ #k < #i)  ∥
       (∃ #k. (Corrupt( s ) @ #k) ∧ #k < #i) )
  case case_1
  solve( (∃ #k. (Signed( $s, K, v2 ) @ #k) ∧ #k < #j)  ∥
         (∃ #k. (Corrupt( $s ) @ #k) ∧ #k < #j) )
    case case_1
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  next
    case case_2
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  qed
next
  case case_2
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
qed

  "∀ s K v1 v2 #i.
    (Evidence( s, K, v1, v2 ) @ #i) ⇒
    (∃ #k. (Corrupt( s ) @ #k) ∧ (#k < #i))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ s K v1 v2 #i.
  (Evidence( s, K, v1, v2 ) @ #i) ∧ ∀ #k. (Corrupt( s ) @ #k) ⇒ ¬(#k < #i)"
*/

lemma B_conflict_needs_2qN_corrupt [hide_lemma=A2_signer_authorized]:
  all-traces
  "∀ c1 c2 K v1 v2 #i #j.
    (((Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ∧
     (¬(v1 = v2))) ⇒
    (∃ s1 s2 #k #l.
      ((Corrupt( s1 ) @ #k) ∧ (Corrupt( s2 ) @ #l)) ∧ (¬(s1 = s2)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ c1 c2 K v1 v2 #i #j.
  (Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j)
 ∧
  (¬(v1 = v2)) ∧
  (∀ s1 s2 #k #l. (Corrupt( s1 ) @ #k) ∧ (Corrupt( s2 ) @ #l) ⇒ s1 = s2)"
*/
simplify
solve( Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i )
  case Accept_012
  solve( !Pk( 's0', pk(skA) ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Pk( 's1', pk(skB) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Pk( 's2', pk(skC) ) ▶₂ #i )
        case Register
        solve( Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j )
          case Accept_012
          solve( (v1 = v2)  ∥
                 (∃ #k. (Corrupt( 's0' ) @ #k) ∧ ((#k < #i) ∨ (#k < #j))) )
            case case_1
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case case_2
            solve( (v1 = v2)  ∥
                   (∃ #k. (Corrupt( 's1' ) @ #k) ∧ ((#k < #i) ∨ (#k < #j))) )
              case case_1
              by contradiction /* from formulas */

          solve( (v1 = v2)  ∥

lemma C1_intersection_nonempty [hide_lemma=A2_signer_authorized]:
  all-traces
  "∀ c1 c2 K v1 v2 #i #j.
    (((Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ∧
     (¬(v1 = v2))) ⇒
    (∃ s. (Verified( s, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Verified( s, K, v2 ) @ #j))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ c1 c2 K v1 v2 #i #j.
  (Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j)
 ∧
  (¬(v1 = v2)) ∧
  (∀ s. (Verified( s, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Verified( s, K, v2 ) @ #j) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i )
  case Accept_012
  solve( !Pk( 's0', pk(skA) ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Pk( 's1', pk(skB) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Pk( 's2', pk(skC) ) ▶₂ #i )
        case Register

lemma C2_intersection_at_least_2qN [hide_lemma=A2_signer_authorized]:
  all-traces
  "∀ c1 c2 K v1 v2 #i #j.
    (((Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ∧
     (¬(v1 = v2))) ⇒
    (∃ s t.
      ((((Verified( s, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Verified( s, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ∧
        (Verified( t, K, v1 ) @ #i)) ∧
       (Verified( t, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ∧
      (¬(s = t)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ c1 c2 K v1 v2 #i #j.
  (Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j)
 ∧
  (¬(v1 = v2)) ∧
  (∀ s t.
    (Verified( s, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧
    (Verified( s, K, v2 ) @ #j) ∧
    (Verified( t, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧
    (Verified( t, K, v2 ) @ #j)
   ⇒
    s = t)"
*/
simplify
solve( Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i )
  case Accept_012
  solve( !Pk( 's0', pk(skA) ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Pk( 's1', pk(skB) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Pk( 's2', pk(skC) ) ▶₂ #i )
        case Register
        by solve( Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j )
      qed
    qed
  qed
next
  case Accept_013
  solve( !Pk( 's0', pk(skA) ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Pk( 's1', pk(skB) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Pk( 's3', pk(skC) ) ▶₂ #i )
        case Register
        by solve( Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j )
      qed
    qed
  qed
next
  case Accept_023
  solve( !Pk( 's0', pk(skA) ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Pk( 's2', pk(skB) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Pk( 's3', pk(skC) ) ▶₂ #i )
        case Register
        by solve( Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j )

                             hide_lemma=D2_intersection_seats_corrupt]:
  exists-trace "∃ s K v1 v2 #i. Evidence( s, K, v1, v2 ) @ #i"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ s K v1 v2 #i. (Evidence( s, K, v1, v2 ) @ #i)"
*/
simplify

  case Accept_012
  solve( !Cert( c2, K, v2 ) ▶₁ #i )
    case Accept_012
    solve( !Signer( ~c, s, sig1 ) ▶₂ #i )
      case Accept_012_case_1
      solve( !Signer( ~c.1, 's0', sig2 ) ▶₃ #i )
        case Accept_012
        solve( !KU( sign(<K, v1, 's0'>, ~sk) ) @ #vk.6 )
          case Sign
          solve( !KU( sign(<K, v1, 's1'>, ~sk.1) ) @ #vk.9 )
            case Sign
            solve( !KU( sign(<K, v1, 's2'>, ~sk.2) ) @ #vk.10 )
              case Sign
              solve( !KU( sign(<K, v2, 's0'>, ~sk) ) @ #vk.14 )
                case c_sign
                solve( !KU( ~sk ) @ #vk.20 )
                  case Corrupt
                  solve( !KU( sign(<K, v2, 's1'>, ~sk.1) ) @ #vk.19 )
                    case c_sign
                    solve( !KU( ~sk.1 ) @ #vk.24 )
                      case Corrupt
                      solve( !KU( sign(<K, v2, 's2'>, ~sk.2) ) @ #vk.23 )
                        case c_sign
                        solve( !KU( ~sk.2 ) @ #vk.28 )
                          case Corrupt
                          SOLVED // trace found
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed

lemma E2_at_threshold_conflict_exists [hide_lemma=A2_signer_authorized,
                                       hide_lemma=D2_intersection_seats_corrupt]:
  exists-trace
  "∃ c1 c2 K v1 v2 s1 s2 #i #j #k #l.
    ((((((Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧ (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j)) ∧
        (¬(v1 = v2))) ∧
       (Corrupt( s1 ) @ #k)) ∧
      (Corrupt( s2 ) @ #l)) ∧
     (¬(s1 = s2))) ∧
    (∀ s #m. (Corrupt( s ) @ #m) ⇒ ((s = s1) ∨ (s = s2)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ c1 c2 K v1 v2 s1 s2 #i #j #k #l.
  (Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i) ∧
  (Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j) ∧
  (Corrupt( s1 ) @ #k) ∧
  (Corrupt( s2 ) @ #l)
 ∧
  (¬(v1 = v2)) ∧
  (¬(s1 = s2)) ∧
  (∀ s #m. (Corrupt( s ) @ #m) ⇒ ((s = s1) ∨ (s = s2)))"
*/
simplify
solve( !Seat( $s, ~sk ) ▶₀ #k )
  case Register
  solve( ($s = 's0')  ∥ ($s = 's1')  ∥ ($s = 's2')  ∥ ($s = 's3') )
    case case_1
    solve( !Seat( $s, ~sk.1 ) ▶₀ #l )
      case Register
      solve( ($s = 's0')  ∥ ($s = 's1')  ∥ ($s = 's2')  ∥ ($s = 's3') )
        case case_2
        solve( Accept( c1, K, v1 ) @ #i )
          case Accept_012
          solve( !Pk( 's0', pk(skA) ) ▶₀ #i )
            case Register
            solve( !Pk( 's1', pk(skB) ) ▶₁ #i )
              case Register
              solve( !Pk( 's2', pk(skC) ) ▶₂ #i )
                case Register
                solve( Accept( c2, K, v2 ) @ #j )
                  case Accept_013
                  solve( !Pk( 's0', pk(skA) ) ▶₀ #j )
                    case Register
                    solve( !Pk( 's1', pk(skB) ) ▶₁ #j )
                      case Register
                      solve( !Pk( 's3', pk(skC) ) ▶₂ #j )
                        case Register
                        solve( !KU( sign(<K, v1, 's0'>, ~sk) ) @ #vk.6 )
                          case Sign
                          solve( !KU( sign(<K, v1, 's1'>, ~sk.1) ) @ #vk.9 )
                            case Sign
                            solve( !KU( sign(<K, v1, 's2'>, ~sk.2) ) @ #vk.10 )
                              case Sign
                              solve( !KU( sign(<K, v2, 's0'>, ~sk) ) @ #vk.14 )
                                case c_sign
                                solve( !KU( sign(<K, v2, 's1'>, ~sk.1) ) @ #vk.18 )
                                  case c_sign
                                  solve( !KU( sign(<K, v2, 's3'>, ~sk.3) ) @ #vk.21 )
                                    case Sign
                                    solve( !KU( ~sk ) @ #vk.23 )
                                      case Corrupt
                                      solve( !KU( ~sk.1 ) @ #vk.25 )
                                        case Corrupt
                                        SOLVED // trace found
                                      qed
                                    qed
                                  qed
                                qed
                              qed
                            qed
                          qed
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed
